# Hubitat Elevation Developer Reference

- Firmware snapshot: `2.5.2.128`
- Schema version: `1`
- Content revision: `91cc17293ee88bcf503b6258e3980f8c5e2bcff3c0dbbaec489cb45d2069f3a8`

This reference is split into one Markdown file per API page. Search by class, method, property, or API page ID, or browse the section indexes.

## Start with these guides

- [Developer overview](https://docs2.hubitat.com/en/developer/overview): Start building apps and drivers for Hubitat Elevation.
- [App overview](https://docs2.hubitat.com/en/developer/app/overview): Learn how Hubitat apps fit together.
- [Build a simple app](https://docs2.hubitat.com/en/developer/app/building-a-simple-app): Create a small app and work with hub data.
- [Build a multipage app](https://docs2.hubitat.com/en/developer/app/building-a-multipage-app): Organize app input across multiple pages.
- [Parent and child apps](https://docs2.hubitat.com/en/developer/app/parent-child-apps): Structure apps with parent and child instances.
- [App preferences](https://docs2.hubitat.com/en/developer/app/preferences): Define settings and preferences for an app.
- [Driver overview](https://docs2.hubitat.com/en/developer/driver/overview): Understand driver structure and lifecycle.
- [Parent and child drivers](https://docs2.hubitat.com/en/developer/driver/parent-child-drivers): Coordinate related devices with parent and child drivers.
- [Library definition](https://docs2.hubitat.com/en/developer/library-definition): Share reusable code through libraries.
- [Allowed imports](https://docs2.hubitat.com/en/developer/allowed-imports): Check which external imports are available.
- [Best practices](https://docs2.hubitat.com/en/developer/best-practices): Follow guidance for reliable apps and drivers.
- [Build a Z-Wave driver](https://docs2.hubitat.com/en/developer/driver/building-a-zwave-driver): Implement a driver for Z-Wave devices.
- [Build a Zigbee driver](https://docs2.hubitat.com/en/developer/driver/building-a-zigbee-driver): Implement a driver for Zigbee devices.
- [Build a Matter driver](https://docs2.hubitat.com/en/developer/driver/building-a-matter-driver): Implement a driver for Matter devices.
- [Build a LAN driver](https://docs2.hubitat.com/en/developer/driver/building-a-lan-driver): Connect local network devices and use HTTP requests.
